          All -> [text:
                    -- | Boolean monoid under conjunction '(&&)'.
--
-- > All x <> All y = All (x && y)
--
-- ==== __Examples__
--
-- >>> All True <> mempty <> All False)
-- All {getAll = False}
--
-- >>> mconcat (map (\x -> All (even x)) [2,4,6,7,8])
-- All {getAll = False}
--
-- >>> All True <> mempty
-- All {getAll = True}
                  identifiers:
                    {libraries/ghc-internal/src/GHC/Internal/Data/Semigroup/Internal.hs:183:41-42}
                    GHC.Classes.&&],
          Alt -> [text:
                    -- | Monoid under '<|>'.
--
-- > Alt l <> Alt r == Alt (l <|> r)
--
-- ==== __Examples__
-- >>> Alt (Just 12) <> Alt (Just 24)
-- Alt {getAlt = Just 12}
--
-- >>> Alt Nothing <> Alt (Just 24)
-- Alt {getAlt = Just 24}
--
-- @since base-4.8.0.0
                  identifiers:
                    {libraries/ghc-internal/src/GHC/Internal/Data/Semigroup/Internal.hs:345:20-22}
                    GHC.Internal.Base.<|>],
          Any -> [text:
                    -- | Boolean monoid under disjunction '(||)'.
--
-- > Any x <> Any y = Any (x || y)
--
-- ==== __Examples__
--
-- >>> Any True <> mempty <> Any False
-- Any {getAny = True}
--
-- >>> mconcat (map (\x -> Any (even x)) [2,4,6,7,8])
-- Any {getAny = True}
--
-- >>> Any False <> mempty
-- Any {getAny = False}
                  identifiers:
                    {libraries/ghc-internal/src/GHC/Internal/Data/Semigroup/Internal.hs:215:41-42}
                    GHC.Classes.||],
          Dual -> [text:
                     -- | The dual of a 'Monoid', obtained by swapping the arguments of '(<>)'.
--
-- > Dual a <> Dual b == Dual (b <> a)
--
-- ==== __Examples__
--
-- >>> Dual "Hello" <> Dual "World"
-- Dual {getDual = "WorldHello"}
--
-- >>> Dual (Dual "Hello") <> Dual (Dual "World")
-- Dual {getDual = Dual {getDual = "HelloWorld"}}
                   identifiers:
                     {libraries/ghc-internal/src/GHC/Internal/Data/Semigroup/Internal.hs:76:21-26}
                     GHC.Internal.Base.Monoid
                     {libraries/ghc-internal/src/GHC/Internal/Data/Semigroup/Internal.hs:76:70-71}
                     GHC.Internal.Base.<>],
          Endo -> [text:
                     -- | The monoid of endomorphisms under composition.
--
-- > Endo f <> Endo g == Endo (f . g)
--
-- ==== __Examples__
--
-- >>> let computation = Endo ("Hello, " ++) <> Endo (++ "!")
-- >>> appEndo computation "Haskell"
-- "Hello, Haskell!"
--
-- >>> let computation = Endo (*3) <> Endo (+1)
-- >>> appEndo computation 1
-- 6
                   identifiers:],
          Product -> [text:
                        -- | Monoid under multiplication.
--
-- > Product x <> Product y == Product (x * y)
--
-- ==== __Examples__
--
-- >>> Product 3 <> Product 4 <> mempty
-- Product {getProduct = 12}
--
-- >>> mconcat [ Product n | n <- [2 .. 10]]
-- Product {getProduct = 3628800}
                      identifiers:],

--
-- >>> mconcat [ Sum n | n <- [3 .. 9]]
-- Sum {getSum = 42}
                  identifiers:],
